# Fact ledger: neighborhoods across the Grand Strand (batch 2026-10-b)

For page 1, "Best neighborhoods in Myrtle Beach, by what you want nearby" (`/buyers/best-neighborhoods/`). The rows also feed pages 2 (gated), 3 (no HOA) and 5 (affordable). One fact, one value: pages 2, 3 and 5 should cite these rows, not re-derive them.

Researcher: batch 2026-10-b researcher for page 1. Every source below was opened on 2026-10-13. Every row is marked "(added 2026-10-13)". The verifier columns are empty. **The writer may use rows marked `verified` only.**

Machine-readable summary: `batches/2026-10-b/data/neighborhoods.json` (one object per neighborhood, with the row numbers).

## Deciding fact (reported first)

The plan needs 8 to 12 named neighborhoods, each with a typical sale price, an HOA fee (or none) and minutes to the beach. Result: **15 neighborhoods have a recorded typical resale price and beach minutes. 13 have an HOA fee from listings.** Two have no HOA fee figure: Cherry Grove (listing cards under one MLS name show none, row 175) and Surfside Beach (no listing under one MLS name). Four price samples are small (Grande Dunes 7 resales, Prince Creek 10, The Farm 16, the Pine Lakes group 17; see Not verified, item 8). The four 55+ pages are included by reference (section "55+ communities by reference").

## Method notes for the verifier

- **Which neighborhoods.** 15 named places across the main areas: Myrtle Beach (Pine Lakes, Grande Dunes, The Market Common), Carolina Forest (The Farm, Berkshire Forest, Plantation Lakes, Waterbridge), Forestbrook (Forestbrook Estates), North Myrtle Beach (Cherry Grove, Tidewater Plantation, Barefoot Resort), Surfside Beach (Deerfield Plantation area; the beach-side streets of the town), Murrells Inlet (Prince Creek) and Conway (Wild Wing Plantation). Chosen for search demand (names in the task and the plan) and for a spread of prices, HOA and no HOA, golf and new construction.
- **Group = the county's name, not the marketing name.** Each neighborhood is the set of county parcels whose legal description carries the name (HorryCountyGISApp layer 24), with the filter in its first row. Several are narrower or wider than the name people use. Read each first row before using the price:
  - *The Farm*: "THE FARM @ TIMBERLAKE" (near Surfside, 265 parcels) and "THE FARM AT FOX BAY" (near Loris) are different places and are left out. Page 4's researcher should use the same 273-parcel set.
  - *Pine Lakes*: the county name "PINE LAKES" covers The Preserve at Pine Lakes (phases 1A, 2, 3; it has an HOA) and Pine Lakes Estates. The older streets by the club are "GOLF COURSE SECT" and similar, and are not in the price.
  - *Grande Dunes*: only the sections whose description names Grande Dunes (The Villages, Bal Harbor, Village, Villa Venezia, Promenade, the parcels east of Grande Dunes Blvd). Most of Grande Dunes is filed under other names (Cipriana, Members Club, Seville, Siena Park, Palermo, Milano, Cadiz and others), and Del Webb is "MARINA TRACT". "GRANDE DUNES N"/"GRANDE DUNES NORTH" (tax district 550, near 33.79, -78.79) is a different place and is left out.
  - *Barefoot Resort*: only parcels named Barefoot Resort or Barefoot Village (Yacht Club Villas, North Tower, Dye Townhomes, The Retreat). Barefoot Resort's many other condo and house sections carry their own names and are not in the price. It is mostly condos.
  - *Surfside Beach*: parcels inside the town limits (OpenData Municipalities) on the ocean side of US 17 Business (county Highways layer 17), by a point-side test on the parcel centroid. It is most of the town.
  - *Prince Creek*: Seasons at Prince Creek (55+, its own page) is left out.
- **Prices.** Same method as batch 2026-10-a (`templates/research-brief.md`): AcclaimWeb Doc Type DEED (001), recording dates 10/1/2025 to 9/30/2026 (30,339 deeds countywide, the same count as batch 2026-10-a; index "Released through date: 10/04/2026"), matched by ParcelNumber. Dropped: consideration under $10,000; corrective or re-recorded deeds; deeds that convey several lots or units (memo or legal says "SEVERAL", "LOTS" or "n & n"); deeds from a town, city or county; tract or remainder parcels; business or industrial units; deeds to a builder. Sales by any company that sold 4 or more in the group, or by a named builder (NVR, D.R. Horton, Lennar, Pulte, Dream Finders, Sands Building Group, RS Parker, Waterbridge SC LLC), are reported apart. Parcels with no building value on the roll are reported apart as "land". The middle half uses quartiles, inclusive method. Lowest and highest sales were opened on their detail pages; all other sales are listed by Book/Page.
- **Types.** condo = land use 107 or "HPR", "U/APT/CONDO" or "UNIT" in the legal description; townhouse = land use 109 or "TWNH"/"TOWNH"; land = MarketImprv 0; house = the rest. The county publishes no land use key online; the codes were read from examples.
- **Reference point.** For each neighborhood, the county address point (layer 22) on a parcel of the group nearest the mean centroid of its home parcels. It is a home address, used only as a start point; the page should say "from the middle of the neighborhood", not the address.
- **Distances.** OSRM public router (router.project-osrm.org), table call to choose, route call for the figure. Beach: S.C. Department of Environmental Services beach access layer, public accesses run by a city, town or county (state parks left out; the layer's records date from 2013 to 2019). Grocery: OpenStreetMap supermarkets of the named chains (Overpass, maps.mail.ru mirror; overpass-api.de reset the connection). Hospital: the four open acute-care hospitals, points reused from batch 2026-10-a (places.json, verified there). McLeod Health Carolina Forest's new hospital was not open by any source found; where it is closer, a separate row says so.
- **Flood.** The county Flood Zones layer 19 (FEMA zones) for every address point, then FEMA's own NFHL layer 28 at the reference point of all 15 and at 4 points inside mapped zones. hazards.fema.gov refused curl from this environment (TLS reset), so the FEMA checks were opened with WebFetch; each URL is a point query that opens in a browser. All 18 FEMA answers matched the county layer: reference points X (Surfside's X shaded, 0.2 percent), except Cherry Grove's, which is AE; the 4 in-zone checks (323 48th Ave N, 122 15th Ave N, 1330 Airforce Ln, and Cherry Grove's reference point) returned AE, SFHA_TF "T".
- **Schools.** Horry County Schools Attendance Zone Lookup layers, point query at the reference point. Names only.
- **HOA fees.** The brief's listing exception: Real Geeks (mbcoastalhomes.realgeeks.com) active listings by MLS neighborhood name, each listing page read for "HOA Fees (Calculated Monthly)", and The Beach Pro Team subdivision cards (crawl delay 10 seconds honored) for the same MLS numbers. robots.txt allows both paths for every agent. Both repeat one MLS field, so agreement checks transcription only. The MLS names used are in each row's URL.
- **Owner names.** County owner names appear only for organizations (associations, builders, golf companies). Builder and seller names in deed rows are companies.
- **Not recorded, by rule:** who lives anywhere, crime, "safe", "family", school ratings.
- **Work files** (scripts and raw pulls): the researcher's scratchpad `nbhd/` folder. The deed grid pulls are reproducible with the AcclaimWeb search named above.

## Waiting for MLS

- Median sale price and days on market by neighborhood from MLS closed sales, to replace or confirm the deed-index medians, mainly where the sample is small (Grande Dunes 7 resales, Prince Creek 10, The Farm 16, Pine Lakes group 17).
- Year built and home sizes by neighborhood (the county roll has no year built; listing pages do, but the brief allows listings for HOA fees only).
- The HOA fee for Cherry Grove and Surfside Beach streets, and the share of homes there with no HOA (page 3 needs it).
- Price per square foot.

## Search check

Not run as a separate step for this ledger. The plan (`batches/2026-10-b/PLAN.md`) lists the searches read on 2026-10-12 and 2026-10-13. The rows answer the plan's must-answer list in this order: price (deed rows), HOA fee (listing rows), minutes to the beach, map point (reference rows), and the trade-offs a buyer meets (flood rows, builder activity, distance to a hospital). Not answered here: "walk or bike to the beach" as a measured walking time (only Cherry Grove and Surfside Beach are under 2 minutes by car; a walking route was not computed).

## Not verified

1. **Amenities (pool, clubhouse, trails) for most neighborhoods.** No HOA or builder page was found in a search for The Farm, Berkshire Forest, Plantation Lakes, Waterbridge, Wild Wing Plantation, Tidewater, Deerfield, Market Common, Forestbrook Estates or Prince Creek. Listing remarks describe pools and clubhouses (for example The Farm "resort style pools, a clubhouse, fitness center"; Waterbridge a lazy river and a clubhouse; Wild Wing a pool complex with a water slide; Plantation Lakes two pools, a clubhouse and a 170-acre lake; Berkshire Forest two amenity centers). Listings are allowed for HOA fees only, so none of this is a row. The "HOA Fee Includes" field on many listings names "Pool(s)" and "Recreation Facilities"; it is quoted inside the fee rows, not stated as a separate claim. What was tried: web search for each HOA name; grandedunes.com and barefootresort.com return 403 to curl and an empty page to WebFetch.
2. **Gated: not verified for any of the 15.** Listing remarks say Tidewater has "24/7 manned, gated security" and one Waterbridge listing says the community is gated, with gate hours. No HOA document, county record or builder page was found. Page 2's researcher should look for a primary source (HOA rules, county GIS gatehouse parcel, or recorded documents).
3. **55+:** none of the 15 was researched as a 55+ community, and no age rule was seen in the sources read. That is not proof that none has one; say nothing about age on the page for these 15.
4. **Golf at Grande Dunes, Wild Wing, Deerfield and Prince Creek.** No course page or county record was read. One listing calls a course near Deerfield "the former Deer Tract golf course". Leave golf out for these four unless a primary source is found.
5. **Builder and years built.** The deed index names the companies selling new homes now (builder rows). Who built the older sections and when was not found in a primary source.
6. **Store addresses.** Three of the chosen grocery stores have no street address in OpenStreetMap (Food Lion near The Market Common, Lowes Foods near The Farm, Food Lion near Barefoot Resort); the nearest county address points are 3890 S Kings Hwy, 215 Fresh Dr and 3924 S Hwy 17. Kroger for Grande Dunes is tagged "1 North Kings Highway" in OSM, which is not a plausible address; the county points nearby are 9600-block N Kings Hwy. Confirm on the chain store pages before naming an address.
7. **McLeod Health Carolina Forest hospital opening.** McLeod's 1 September 2026 release says "scheduled to open in the coming weeks". No source found that it has opened. Rows 9, 43, 54, 66, 79 give the drive only.
8. **Small samples.** Grande Dunes (7 resales, all over $1.19 million, while builders sold 54 new homes there with a middle of $678,500), Prince Creek (10), The Farm (16) and the Pine Lakes group (17). Say "few resales" or give the range.
9. **The Farm low sale.** The lowest The Farm sale, $140,000 (Book 5029, Page 1662), is between two people with the same last name and may not be an open-market sale. The method keeps every deed of $10,000 or more, as in batch 2026-10-a; the middle sale moves little without it. The same holds for $70,000 in Pine Lakes (5106/827), $70,000 in Deerfield (5113/87) and $55,000 in Cherry Grove (5053/1989).
10. **Surfside Beach HOA.** The beach-side streets of the town have no single MLS neighborhood name; no listing-based fee was gathered. County records show small associations inside the town (row 139).
11. **Cherry Grove flood.** 1,929 of 2,057 address points are in Zone AE and 84 in VE. That is the main trade-off there and needs no hedging, but flood insurance costs were not researched.

## Questions only the brokerage can answer

1. Which of these 15 do Chapter3 agents show most, and is any well-known name missing (for example Arcadian Shores, Prestwick, Indigo Creek, Avalon, Emmens Preserve or Withers Preserve, which were counted but not written up)?
2. For Grande Dunes and Barefoot Resort, which county section names do agents treat as part of the community? A list would let the price rows cover the whole community.
3. Are any of these gated with a staffed gate or a code gate today (Tidewater, Waterbridge, Grande Dunes sections, Barefoot sections)?
4. The HOA fee, and whether there is one at all, for Cherry Grove and Surfside Beach beach-side homes, from Chapter3 closing files or MLS.
5. Does Chapter3 want the McLeod Carolina Forest hospital named once it opens? It would be the nearest hospital for every Carolina Forest neighborhood here.
